    <script>
        // Sync Callback Tracker
        var syncSubmit = false;

        olvrmsgConfig = {
            callback: function(data) {
                if (data.error) {} else {
                    // Email Signup Redirect
                    if ($(syncSubmit).hasClass('email__signup')) {
                        var pUrl = "https://secure.actblue.com/donate/julie-homepage?refcode={FORMTYPE}-homepage";
                        pUrl = pUrl.replace("{FORMTYPE}", "emailsignup");
                        window.location.href = pUrl;
                        // SMS Signup Redirect
                    } else if ($(syncSubmit).hasClass('sms-signup__form')) {
                        var eUrl = "https://secure.actblue.com/donate/julie-homepage?refcode={FORMTYPE}-homepage";
                        eUrl = eUrl.replace("{FORMTYPE}", "smssignup");
                        window.location.href = eUrl;
                        // Default Redirect
                    } else {
                        window.location.href = "https://secure.actblue.com/donate/julie-homepage?refcode={FORMTYPE}-homepage";
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    </script>

    <!-- new fs sign-up form -->
    <section class="new-fs-form-section">
      <div class="new-fs-form-wrapper">

      <div class="container">
        <div class="new-fs-form-kickers">
          <div class="row">
            <div class="col-xs-12">              
              <h2 class="new-fs-form-kickers-text-head">JOIN THE CAMPAIGN</h2>

              <h6 class="new-fs-form-kickers-text">Sign up for info and updates:</h6>

            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

      <div class="big-row row">


        <div class="new-fs-join-signup">
          <div class="email__form new-fs-email">

          <script type="text/javascript">
            var submitted = false;
          </script>

          <iframe name="hidden_iframe" id="hidden_iframe" style="display:none;" onload="if(submitted) {window.location='https://secure.actblue.com/donate/julie-signup?refcode=emailsignup-homepage';}"></iframe>

          <form class='email__signup' method='POST' target='hidden_iframe' enctype='multipart/form-data' id='act' name='email__signup' onsubmit='submitted=true;'><input type='hidden' name='form-name' value='email__signup' />

          <p style="display:none;">
            <label>Don’t fill this out: <input name="bot-field"></label>
          </p>

          <div class="new-fs-fieldset-container">
            <div class="row">
              <fieldset id="signup">     
                  <div class="col new-fs-email-input">
                    <legend><span>Email Address</span></legend>
                    <div class="text">
                      <label for="email"><span class="email__form--label">Email Address</span>
                        <input type="email" id="email" name="email" placeholder="you@yourdomain.com">
                     </label>
                    </div>
                  </div>
              </fieldset>

              <fieldset id="address">
                  <div class="col">
                    <legend><span>Zip Code</span></legend>
                    <div class="text new-fs-input">
                      <label for="zip-address"><span class="email__form--ziplabel">Zip Code</span>
                      <input id="zip-address" placeholder="Your 5 digit zip code" type="text" name="zip-address" maxlength="5">
                    </label>
                  </div>
                  </div>
              </fieldset>
            </div> <!-- row --> 

             <div class="row">
              <div class="submit new-fs-form-submit-btn">
                <input type="hidden" name="tags" value="websiteemail" />
                <input type="hidden" name="page" value="webpage-signup-julieoliverorg">
                <input class="submit" type="submit" value="Submit">
              </div> 
            </div> <!-- row -->        
        </div> <!-- new-fs-fieldset-container -->

        </form> <!-- form -->
        </div> <!-- email__form -->
        </div> <!-- new-fs-join-signup -->
   
        
       
          <div class="new-fs-svg-container">
            <!-- <svg class="events__form--icon">
              <use xlink:href="#cd25-map"></use>
            </svg> -->
            <img class="lazyload" data-srcset="./assets/img/2x/cd25-map@2x-8.png" alt="Texas Congressional District 25">
          </div>      
     
      <!-- </div> big row -->
      </div> <!-- container -->
      </div><!--  new wrapper -->

    <section class="marquee" role="main" id="content">
        <div class="email-join">
        <h1 class="join-signup__hed">JOIN THE CAMPAIGN.</h1>
        <div class="email__form olvrmsg-wrapper">

                <script type="text/javascript">
                    var submitted = false;
                </script>

                <iframe name="hidden_iframe" id="hidden_iframe" style="display:none;" onload="if(submitted) {window.location='https://secure.actblue.com/donate/julie-signup?refcode=emailsignup-homepage';}"></iframe>

                <form class="email__signup" method="post" action="https://docs.google.com/forms/d/e/1FAIpQLSdZ2rjX9KbOVRfasJADiLfayPxyKrekbpQkplzVt04o49i5uw/formResponse" target="hidden_iframe" onsubmit="submitted=true;">
                    <fieldset id="signup">
                        <legend><span>Email Address</span></legend>
                        <div class="text">
                            <label for="email"><span class="email__form--label">Email Address</span>
                                <input type="email" id="email" name="entry.2005620554" placeholder="you@yourdomain.com">
                            </label>
                        </div>
                    </fieldset>
                    <fieldset id="address">
                        <legend><span>Zip Code</span></legend>
                        <div class="text">
                            <label for="zip-address"><span class="email__form--ziplabel">Zip Code</span>
                                <input id="zip-address" placeholder="Your 5 digit zip code" type="text" name="entry.1045781291" maxlength="5">
                            </label>
                        </div>
                    </fieldset>
                    <div class="submit">
                        <input type="hidden" name="tags" value="websiteemail" />
                        <input type="hidden" name="page" value="webpage-signup-julieoliverorg">
                        <input class="olvrmsg-submit-btn" type="submit" value="Submit">
                    </div>
                </form>
                <div class="olvrmsg-fdbk">
                    <div class="olvrmsg-loading" style="display: none;">...loading</div>
                    <div class="olvrmsg-success" style="display: none;">Thanks for signing up!</div>
                </div>
            </div>
    </section>

    <section class="marquee mobile" id="volunteer-mobile" role="main">
        <div class="vol-signup vol__form join-signup">
            <h1 class="vol-signup__hed">Get Involved</h1>
            <p class="vol-signup__sub">Search by zip code to find volunteer opportunities near you!</p>
            <label for="vol-zip-address"><span class="vol__form--label">Zip Code</span>
                <input id="vol-zip-address" placeholder="Your 5 digit zip code" type="text" name="vol-zip" maxlength="5" required>
            </label>
            <button class="olvrmsg-submit-btn" type="button" id="vol-map">Volunteer</button>
            <script type="text/javascript">
                zipMe();

                function zipMe() {
                    var map, zip;
                    map = 'https://map.julieoliver.org/?zipcode=';
                    jQuery('#vol-map').click(function() {
                        zip = jQuery('#vol-zip-address').val();
                        if (zip.length == 5) {
                            window.location.href = map + zip;
                        } else {
                            return false;
                        }
                    })
                }
            </script>
        </div>

<section class="videos meet__julie">
  <div class="videos__wrapper">
    <section class="youtube-kickers">
      <section class="youtube-box">
      <h1 class="sms-signup__hed">Meet Julie </h1>
      <p class = "youtube-box__dek">Julie was born poor in south Dallas, grew up working class in a little town called Ovilla, experienced homelessness as a teenager, and relied on Medicaid for prenatal care when she was seventeen years old. 
      <p class="youtube-box__dek">She knows the struggles we all face, and that's why Julie got into this fight -- before anyone thought it was a fight that could be won.</p>

      <p class="youtube-box__dek">Fighting for and with this community is why she is running, and it's why she's been a steadfast advocate for healthcare for those who need it most in our community.</p>

      <p class="youtube-box__dek"><a href="https://julieoliver.org/about">Read more about Julie.</a></p>
      
      <div class="youtube-wrapper">
        <div class="iframe-container">
          <iframe width="560" height="315" src="https://www.youtube.com/embed/FqZcpwknUo8" frameborder="0" allow="accelerometer; autoplay; encrypted-media; gyroscope; picture-in-picture" allowfullscreen></iframe>
        </div>
      </div>
    
    </section>
  </section>
  </div>
</section>

<section class="videos map__wrapper">
  <div class="videos__wrapper">
    <section class="youtube-kickers">
      <section class="youtube-box">
      <h1 class="sms-signup__hed">Get Involved. </h1>
      <p class="youtube-box__dek">Search to find volunteer opportunities near you!<a href="https://map.julieoliver.org"> Explore map.</a></p>

        <div class="map-iframe-container">
          <iframe width="560" height="315" src="https://map.julieoliver.org" ></iframe>
        </div>

    
    </section>
  </section>
  </div>
</section>

<section class="videos">
    <div class="videos__wrapper">
        <section class="cta-kickers">
            <section class="sms-signup">
                <h1 class="sms-signup__hed">Mobile Alerts </h1>
                <p class="sms-signup__dek">Get updates from straight to your phone or mobile device. </p>
                <br />
                <p class="sms-signup__dek">Sign up for mobile updates today.* </p> 
<!-- 
                <form class="sms-signup__form olvrmsg-form" id ="sms-form" method="POST" data-netlify="true" name="sms-form" target="hidden_iframe" onsubmit="submitted=true;"> -->
                  <form class='sms-signup__form olvrmsg-form' id='sms-form' method='POST' name='sms-form' target='hidden_iframe' onsubmit='submitted=true;'><input type='hidden' name='form-name' value='sms-form' />
                
                  <label>
                    <span class="sms-signup__form--label">Cell Phone Number</span>
                    <input type="text" id="cellphone" name="phone" placeholder="Cell Phone Number" />
                  </label>   
                  <br />
                  <input class="olvrmsg-submit-btn signup-button" name="sms-form-submit-button" id="olvrmsg-submit-btn" type="submit" value="Submit"/>

              </form>

              <p class="successMsg" role="alert" hidden>Thank you! Number submitted...</p>
              <p class="errorMsg" role="alert" hidden>Something went wrong...</p>

                <script type="text/javascript">var submitted=false;</script>
                <iframe name="hidden_iframe" id="hidden_iframe" style="display:none;" 
                onload="if(submitted) {window.location='https://secure.actblue.com/donate/julie-signup?refcode=emailsignup-homepage';}"></iframe>
            </section>

            <section class="donate">
                <h1 class="donate__hed">Make a Donation</h1>
                <p class="donate__dek">Our campaign doesn't take a single dime from any PACs or special interest groups, so your donations really do count. Chip in today to power our grassroots campaign for Congress.</p>
                <form id="donate_form" class="donate__form">
                    <fieldset>
                        <legend><span>Donation Amounts</span></legend>
                        <input type="radio" id="donate_15" class="donate__radio" name="donate__amount" value="15">
                        <label for="donate_15" class="donate__label">$15</label>
                        <input type="radio" id="donate_25" class="donate__radio" name="donate__amount" value="25">
                        <label for="donate_25" class="donate__label">$25</label>
                        <input type="radio" id="donate_50" class="donate__radio" name="donate__amount" value="50">
                        <label for="donate_50" class="donate__label">$50</label>
                        <input type="radio" id="donate_other" class="donate__radio" name="donate__amount" value="other">
                        <label for="donate_other" class="donate__label">Other</label>
                    </fieldset>
                    <a href="https://secure.actblue.com/donate/julie-homepage?amount=10&amp;refcode=module-homepage" class="donate__link button">Donate</a>
                </form>
            </section>
        </section>
    </div>
</section>

<text transform="matrix(0.9962 -8.699845e-02 8.699845e-02 0.9962 8.3544 348.4958)" class="stmap9 stmap10 stmap11">DISTRICT 25</text>
</symbol>

    </svg>
</div>
<script>
  if (window.netlifyIdentity) {
    window.netlifyIdentity.on("init", user => {
      if (!user) {
        window.netlifyIdentity.on("login", () => {
          document.location.href = "/admin/";
        });
      }
    });
  }


  const form = document.querySelector('form[name="sms-form"]');
  form.addEventListener('submit', async event => {
    event.preventDefault();
    
    // disable button to prevent multiple submissions
    document.querySelector('input[name="sms-form-submit-button"]').disabled = true;

    // make the request to submit the form
    try {
      const response = await fetch('/', {
        method: 'post',
        headers: {
          'Content-type': 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded; charset=UTF-8'
        },
        // parse and submit all included form data
        body: new URLSearchParams(new FormData(form)).toString(),
        redirect: 'follow'
      })
      .then(response => {
        window.location.href = "https://secure.actblue.com/donate/julie-signup?refcode=emailsignup-homepage"
      }).catch(error => {
        console.error('Error: ', error)
      });

      // if it was successful show success message
      if (response.status === 200) {
        document.querySelector('.successMsg').hidden = false;
      } else {
        document.querySelector('.errorMsg').hidden = false;
      }
    } catch (e) {
      console.error(e);
    }
  });


</script>
</body>
</html>
